+std::vector<size_t> makeBlockOffsets(const size_t total, const int parts) {
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets(static_cast<size_t>(parts) + 1, 0);
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+
+    for (int part = 0; part < parts; ++part) {
+        offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part) + 1] =
+            offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part)] + base +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(part) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return offsets;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const std::vector<size_t>& offsets, const size_t index) {
+    return static_cast<int>(std::upper_bound(offsets.begin(), offsets.end(), index) -
+                            offsets.begin()) -
+           1;
+}
+
+void sendUnsigned(const unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int destination, const int tag,
+                  MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, destination, tag,
+                 communicator);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void receiveUnsigned(unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void copyGlobalBlockToLocal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& global,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& local, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes), columnCount,
+                    local.data() + localRow * columnCount);
+    }
+}
+
+void copyLocalBlockToGlobal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& global, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(local.data() + localRow * columnCount, columnCount,
+                    global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes));
+    }
+}
+
+void distributeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance, const size_t numNodes,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& rowOffsets,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& columnOffsets, MPI_Comm grid,
+                              const int gridRank, const int gridSize) {
+    if (gridRank == 0) {
+        std::vector<unsigned int> globalDistance(numNodes * numNodes);
+        initializeDistanceMatrix(globalDistance, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
+
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < gridSize; ++rank) {
+            int coordinates[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid, rank, 2, coordinates);
+            const size_t rowOffset = rowOffsets[coordinates[0]];
+            const size_t rowCount = rowOffsets[coordinates[0] + 1] - rowOffset;
+            const size_t columnOffset = columnOffsets[coordinates[1]];
+            const size_t columnCount = columnOffsets[coordinates[1] + 1] - columnOffset;
+
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                copyGlobalBlockToLocal(globalDistance, localDistance, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                       columnOffset, columnCount);
+            } else {
+                std::vector<unsigned int> packed(rowCount * columnCount);
+                copyGlobalBlockToLocal(globalDistance, packed, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                       columnOffset, columnCount);
+                sendUnsigned(packed.data(), packed.size(), rank, 0, grid);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        receiveUnsigned(localDistance.data(), localDistance.size(), 0, 0, grid);
+    }
+}
+
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance,
+                                                const size_t numNodes,
+                                                const std::vector<size_t>& rowOffsets,
+                                                const std::vector<size_t>& columnOffsets,
+                                                MPI_Comm grid, const int gridRank,
+                                                const int gridSize) {
+    if (gridRank != 0) {
+        sendUnsigned(localDistance.data(), localDistance.size(), 0, 1, grid);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<unsigned int> globalDistance(numNodes * numNodes);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < gridSize; ++rank) {
+        int coordinates[2];
+        MPI_Cart_coords(grid, rank, 2, coordinates);
+        const size_t rowOffset = rowOffsets[coordinates[0]];
+        const size_t rowCount = rowOffsets[coordinates[0] + 1] - rowOffset;
+        const size_t columnOffset = columnOffsets[coordinates[1]];
+        const size_t columnCount = columnOffsets[coordinates[1] + 1] - columnOffset;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            copyLocalBlockToGlobal(localDistance, globalDistance, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                   columnOffset, columnCount);
+        } else {
+            std::vector<unsigned int> packed(rowCount * columnCount);
+            receiveUnsigned(packed.data(), packed.size(), rank, 1, grid);
+            copyLocalBlockToGlobal(packed, globalDistance, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                   columnOffset, columnCount);
         }
-        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
     }
+    return globalDistance;
+}

+void initializeLocalPathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t localSourceCount,
+                               const size_t localSourceOffset,
+                               const size_t localDestinationCount) {
+    if (localSourceCount == 0 || localDestinationCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < localSourceCount; ++localSource) {
+        std::fill_n(path.data() + localSource * localDestinationCount, localDestinationCount,
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(localSourceOffset + localSource));
+    }
 }

+std::vector<size_t> makeBlockOffsets(const size_t total, const int parts) {
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets(static_cast<size_t>(parts) + 1, 0);
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+
+    for (int part = 0; part < parts; ++part) {
+        offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part) + 1] =
+            offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part)] + base +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(part) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return offsets;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const std::vector<size_t>& offsets, const size_t index) {
+    return static_cast<int>(std::upper_bound(offsets.begin(), offsets.end(), index) -
+                            offsets.begin()) -
+           1;
+}
+
+void sendUnsigned(const unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int destination, const int tag,
+                  MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, destination, tag,
+                 communicator);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void receiveUnsigned(unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void copyGlobalBlockToLocal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& global,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& local, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes), columnCount,
+                    local.data() + localRow * columnCount);
+    }
+}
+
+void copyLocalBlockToGlobal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& global, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(local.data() + localRow * columnCount, columnCount,
+                    global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes));
+    }
+}
+
+void distributeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance, const size_t numNodes,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& rowOffsets,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& columnOffsets, MPI_Comm grid,
+                              const int gridRank, const int gridSize) {
+    if (gridRank == 0) {
+        std::vector<unsigned int> globalDistance(numNodes * numNodes);
+        initializeDistanceMatrix(globalDistance, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
+
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < gridSize; ++rank) {
+            int coordinates[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid, rank, 2, coordinates);
+            const size_t rowOffset = rowOffsets[coordinates[0]];
+            const size_t rowCount = rowOffsets[coordinates[0] + 1] - rowOffset;
+            const size_t columnOffset = columnOffsets[coordinates[1]];
+            const size_t columnCount = columnOffsets[coordinates[1] + 1] - columnOffset;
+
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                copyGlobalBlockToLocal(globalDistance, localDistance, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                       columnOffset, columnCount);
+            } else {
+                std::vector<unsigned int> packed(rowCount * columnCount);
+                copyGlobalBlockToLocal(globalDistance, packed, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                       columnOffset, columnCount);
+                sendUnsigned(packed.data(), packed.size(), rank, 0, grid);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        receiveUnsigned(localDistance.data(), localDistance.size(), 0, 0, grid);
+    }
+}
+
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance,
+                                                const size_t numNodes,
+                                                const std::vector<size_t>& rowOffsets,
+                                                const std::vector<size_t>& columnOffsets,
+                                                MPI_Comm grid, const int gridRank,

+        sendUnsigned(localDistance.data(), localDistance.size(), 0, 1, grid);
+        return {};

+

+        const size_t columnOffset = columnOffsets[coordinates[1]];
+        const size_t columnCount = columnOffsets[coordinates[1] + 1] - columnOffset;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {

+                                   columnOffset, columnCount);
+        } else {
+            std::vector<unsigned int> packed(rowCount * columnCount);
+            receiveUnsigned(packed.data(), packed.size(), rank, 1, grid);
+            copyLocalBlockToGlobal(packed, globalDistance, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                   columnOffset, columnCount);
         }
-        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
     }
+    return globalDistance;
+}

+void initializeLocalPathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t localSourceCount,
+                               const size_t localSourceOffset,
+                               const size_t localDestinationCount) {

+        std::fill_n(path.data() + localSource * localDestinationCount, localDestinationCount,
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(localSourceOffset + localSource));
+    }

+std::vector<size_t> makeBlockOffsets(const size_t total, const int parts) {
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets(static_cast<size_t>(parts) + 1, 0);
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+
+    for (int part = 0; part < parts; ++part) {
+        offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part) + 1] =
+            offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part)] + base +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(part) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return offsets;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const std::vector<size_t>& offsets, const size_t index) {
+    return static_cast<int>(std::upper_bound(offsets.begin(), offsets.end(), index) -
+                            offsets.begin()) -
+           1;
+}
+
+void sendUnsigned(const unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int destination, const int tag,
+                  MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, destination, tag,
+                 communicator);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void receiveUnsigned(unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void copyGlobalBlockToLocal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& global,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& local, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes), columnCount,
+                    local.data() + localRow * columnCount);
+    }
+}
+
+void copyLocalBlockToGlobal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& global, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(local.data() + localRow * columnCount, columnCount,
+                    global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes));
+    }
+}
+
+void distributeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance, const size_t numNodes,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& rowOffsets,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& columnOffsets, MPI_Comm grid,
+                              const int gridRank, const int gridSize) {
+    if (gridRank == 0) {
+        std::vector<unsigned int> globalDistance(numNodes * numNodes);
+        initializeDistanceMatrix(globalDistance, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
+
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < gridSize; ++rank) {
+            int coordinates[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid, rank, 2, coordinates);
+            const size_t rowOffset = rowOffsets[coordinates[0]];
+            const size_t rowCount = rowOffsets[coordinates[0] + 1] - rowOffset;
+            const size_t columnOffset = columnOffsets[coordinates[1]];
+            const size_t columnCount = columnOffsets[coordinates[1] + 1] - columnOffset;
+
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                copyGlobalBlockToLocal(globalDistance, localDistance, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                       columnOffset, columnCount);
+            } else {
+                std::vector<unsigned int> packed(rowCount * columnCount);
+                copyGlobalBlockToLocal(globalDistance, packed, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                       columnOffset, columnCount);
+                sendUnsigned(packed.data(), packed.size(), rank, 0, grid);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        receiveUnsigned(localDistance.data(), localDistance.size(), 0, 0, grid);
+    }
+}
+
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance,
+                                                const size_t numNodes,

+                                   columnOffset, columnCount);
         }

+    return globalDistance;

-void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {

+std::vector<size_t> makeBlockOffsets(const size_t total, const int parts) {
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets(static_cast<size_t>(parts) + 1, 0);
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+
+    for (int part = 0; part < parts; ++part) {
+        offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part) + 1] =
+            offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part)] + base +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(part) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return offsets;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const std::vector<size_t>& offsets, const size_t index) {
+    return static_cast<int>(std::upper_bound(offsets.begin(), offsets.end(), index) -
+                            offsets.begin()) -
+           1;
+}
+
+void sendUnsigned(const unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int destination, const int tag,
+                  MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, destination, tag,
+                 communicator);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void receiveUnsigned(unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void copyGlobalBlockToLocal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& global,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& local, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes), columnCount,
+                    local.data() + localRow * columnCount);
+    }
+}
+
+void copyLocalBlockToGlobal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& global, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(local.data() + localRow * columnCount, columnCount,
+                    global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes));
+    }
+}
+
+void distributeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance, const size_t numNodes,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& rowOffsets,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& columnOffsets, MPI_Comm grid,
+                              const int gridRank, const int gridSize) {
+    if (gridRank == 0) {
+        std::vector<unsigned int> globalDistance(numNodes * numNodes);
+        initializeDistanceMatrix(globalDistance, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
+
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < gridSize; ++rank) {
+            int coordinates[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid, rank, 2, coordinates);
+            const size_t rowOffset = rowOffsets[coordinates[0]];
+            const size_t rowCount = rowOffsets[coordinates[0] + 1] - rowOffset;
+            const size_t columnOffset = columnOffsets[coordinates[1]];
+            const size_t columnCount = columnOffsets[coordinates[1] + 1] - columnOffset;
+
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                copyGlobalBlockToLocal(globalDistance, localDistance, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                       columnOffset, columnCount);
+            } else {
+                std::vector<unsigned int> packed(rowCount * columnCount);
+                copyGlobalBlockToLocal(globalDistance, packed, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                       columnOffset, columnCount);
+                sendUnsigned(packed.data(), packed.size(), rank, 0, grid);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        receiveUnsigned(localDistance.data(), localDistance.size(), 0, 0, grid);
+    }
+}
+
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance,
+                                                const size_t numNodes,
+                                                const std::vector<size_t>& rowOffsets,
+                                                const std::vector<size_t>& columnOffsets,
+                                                MPI_Comm grid, const int gridRank,
+                                                const int gridSize) {
+    if (gridRank != 0) {
+        sendUnsigned(localDistance.data(), localDistance.size(), 0, 1, grid);
+        return {};
+    }
+
+    std::vector<unsigned int> globalDistance(numNodes * numNodes);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < gridSize; ++rank) {
+        int coordinates[2];
+        MPI_Cart_coords(grid, rank, 2, coordinates);
+        const size_t rowOffset = rowOffsets[coordinates[0]];
+        const size_t rowCount = rowOffsets[coordinates[0] + 1] - rowOffset;
+        const size_t columnOffset = columnOffsets[coordinates[1]];
+        const size_t columnCount = columnOffsets[coordinates[1] + 1] - columnOffset;
+
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            copyLocalBlockToGlobal(localDistance, globalDistance, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                   columnOffset, columnCount);
+        } else {
+            std::vector<unsigned int> packed(rowCount * columnCount);
+            receiveUnsigned(packed.data(), packed.size(), rank, 1, grid);
+            copyLocalBlockToGlobal(packed, globalDistance, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                   columnOffset, columnCount);
         }
-        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
     }
+    return globalDistance;
+}
+
+void initializeLocalPathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t localSourceCount,
+                               const size_t localSourceOffset,
+                               const size_t localDestinationCount) {
+    if (localSourceCount == 0 || localDestinationCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < localSourceCount; ++localSource) {
+        std::fill_n(path.data() + localSource * localDestinationCount, localDestinationCount,
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(localSourceOffset + localSource));
+    }
 }

   225	        for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < localSourceCount; ++localSource) {
   226	            const unsigned int sourceToPivot = distanceToPivot[localSource];
   227	            unsigned int* const distanceRow =
   228	                localDistance + localSource * localDestinationCount;
   229	            unsigned int* const pathRow = localPath + localSource * localDestinationCount;
   230	
   231	#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
   232	#pragma GCC ivdep
   233	#endif
   234	            for (size_t localDestination = 0; localDestination < localDestinationCount;
   235	                 ++localDestination) {
   236	                const unsigned int newDistance =
   237	                    sourceToPivot + distanceFromPivot[localDestination];
   238	                if (newDistance < distanceRow[localDestination]) {
   239	                    distanceRow[localDestination] = newDistance;
   240	                    pathRow[localDestination] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
   241	                }
   242	            }
   243	        }
   244	    }
   245	}
   246	
   247	bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
   248	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
   249	        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
   250	            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
   251	            return false;
   252	        }
   253	    }
   254	
   255	    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
   256	        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
   257	            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
   258	                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
   259	                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
   260	                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
   261	
   262	                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF && distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
   263	                    printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", i,
   264	                           j, k);
   265	                    return false;
   266	                }
   267	            }
   268	        }
   269	    }
   270	
   271	    return true;
   272	}
   273	
   274	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   275	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   276	    printf("Options:\n");
   277	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
   278	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   279	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   280	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   281	}
   282	
   283	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   284	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   285	
   286	    int worldSize = 0;
   287	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   288	
   289	    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
   290	    MPI_Dims_create(worldSize, 2, dimensions);
   291	    const int periods[2] = {0, 0};
   292	    MPI_Comm grid = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   293	    MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2, dimensions, periods, 0, &grid);
   294	
   295	    int gridRank = 0;
   296	    int gridSize = 0;
   297	    MPI_Comm_rank(grid, &gridRank);
   298	    MPI_Comm_size(grid, &gridSize);
   299	
   300	    int coordinates[2];
   301	    MPI_Cart_coords(grid, gridRank, 2, coordinates);
   302	
   303	    MPI_Comm columnCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   304	    MPI_Comm rowCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   305	    MPI_Comm_split(grid, coordinates[1], coordinates[0], &columnCommunicator);
   306	    MPI_Comm_split(grid, coordinates[0], coordinates[1], &rowCommunicator);
   307	
   308	    size_t numNodes = 512;
   309	    bool validate = false;
   310	    bool printResults = false;
   311	
   312	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   313	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   314	            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
   315	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   316	            validate = true;
   317	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   318	            printResults = true;
   319	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   320	            if (gridRank == 0) {
   321	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   322	            }
   323	            MPI_Comm_free(&rowCommunicator);
   324	            MPI_Comm_free(&columnCommunicator);
   325	            MPI_Comm_free(&grid);
   326	            MPI_Finalize();
   327	            return 0;
   328	        } else {
   329	            if (gridRank == 0) {
   330	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   331	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   332	            }
   333	            MPI_Comm_free(&rowCommunicator);
   334	            MPI_Comm_free(&columnCommunicator);
   335	            MPI_Comm_free(&grid);
   336	            MPI_Finalize();
   337	            return 1;
   338	        }
   339	    }
   340	
   341	    if (numNodes > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   342	        if (gridRank == 0) {
   343	            printf("Number of nodes exceeds the MPI message-size limit\n");
   344	        }
   345	        MPI_Comm_free(&rowCommunicator);
   346	        MPI_Comm_free(&columnCommunicator);
   347	        MPI_Comm_free(&grid);
   348	        MPI_Finalize();
   349	        return 1;
   350	    }
   351	
   352	    if (gridRank == 0) {
   353	        printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
   354	        printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
   355	        printf("MPI ranks: %d (%d x %d process grid)\n", gridSize, dimensions[0], dimensions[1]);
   356	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   357	        printf("Initializing graph...\n");
   358	    }
   359	
   360	    const std::vector<size_t> rowOffsets = makeBlockOffsets(numNodes, dimensions[0]);
   361	    const std::vector<size_t> columnOffsets = makeBlockOffsets(numNodes, dimensions[1]);
   362	    const size_t localRowOffset = rowOffsets[coordinates[0]];
   363	    const size_t localRowCount = rowOffsets[coordinates[0] + 1] - localRowOffset;
   364	    const size_t localColumnOffset = columnOffsets[coordinates[1]];
   365	    const size_t localColumnCount = columnOffsets[coordinates[1] + 1] - localColumnOffset;
   366	
   367	    std::vector<unsigned int> distance(localRowCount * localColumnCount);
   368	    std::vector<unsigned int> path(localRowCount * localColumnCount);
   369	    distributeDistanceMatrix(distance, numNodes, rowOffsets, columnOffsets, grid, gridRank,
   370	                             gridSize);
   371	    initializeLocalPathMatrix(path, localRowCount, localRowOffset, localColumnCount);
   372	
   373	    if (gridRank == 0) {
   374	        printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
   375	    }
   376	
   377	    MPI_Barrier(grid);
   378	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   379	    floydWarshallDistributed(distance, path, numNodes, localRowOffset, localRowCount,
   380	                             localColumnOffset, localColumnCount, rowOffsets, columnOffsets,
   381	                             coordinates[0], coordinates[1], columnCommunicator, rowCommunicator);
   382	    const double localElapsedSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   383	
   384	    double elapsedSeconds = 0.0;
   385	    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsedSeconds, &elapsedSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, grid);
   386	
   387	    if (gridRank == 0) {
   388	        const long elapsedMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
   389	        printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", elapsedMilliseconds);
   390	        const double operations = static_cast<double>(numNodes) * numNodes * numNodes;
   391	        const double gops = elapsedSeconds > 0.0 ? operations / elapsedSeconds / 1e9 : 0.0;
   392	        printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gops);
   393	    }
   394	
   395	    std::vector<unsigned int> globalDistance;
   396	    if (printResults || validate) {
   397	        globalDistance = gatherDistanceMatrix(distance, numNodes, rowOffsets, columnOffsets, grid,
   398	                                              gridRank, gridSize);
   399	    }
   400	
   401	    int exitCode = 0;
   402	    if (gridRank == 0 && printResults) {
   403	        print_results_int(globalDistance, "DistanceMatrix");
   404	    }
   405	    if (gridRank == 0 && validate) {
   406	        printf("Validating result...\n");
   407	        if (validateResult(globalDistance, numNodes)) {
   408	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   409	        } else {
   410	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   411	            exitCode = 1;
   412	        }
   413	    }
   414	
   415	    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, grid);
   416	    MPI_Comm_free(&rowCommunicator);
   417	    MPI_Comm_free(&columnCommunicator);
   418	    MPI_Comm_free(&grid);
   419	    MPI_Finalize();
   420	    return exitCode;
   421	}

+std::vector<size_t> makeBlockOffsets(const size_t total, const int parts) {
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets(static_cast<size_t>(parts) + 1, 0);
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+
+    for (int part = 0; part < parts; ++part) {
+        offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part) + 1] =
+            offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part)] + base +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(part) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return offsets;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const std::vector<size_t>& offsets, const size_t index) {
+    return static_cast<int>(std::upper_bound(offsets.begin(), offsets.end(), index) -
+                            offsets.begin()) -
+           1;
+}
+
+void sendUnsigned(const unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int destination, const int tag,
+                  MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, destination, tag,
+                 communicator);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void receiveUnsigned(unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void copyGlobalBlockToLocal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& global,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& local, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes), columnCount,
+                    local.data() + localRow * columnCount);
+    }
+}
+
+void copyLocalBlockToGlobal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& global, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(local.data() + localRow * columnCount, columnCount,
+                    global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes));
+    }
+}
+
+void distributeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance, const size_t numNodes,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& rowOffsets,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& columnOffsets, MPI_Comm grid,
+                              const int gridRank, const int gridSize) {
+    if (gridRank == 0) {
+        std::vector<unsigned int> globalDistance(numNodes * numNodes);
+        initializeDistanceMatrix(globalDistance, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
+
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < gridSize; ++rank) {
+            int coordinates[2];
+            MPI_Cart_coords(grid, rank, 2, coordinates);
+            const size_t rowOffset = rowOffsets[coordinates[0]];
+            const size_t rowCount = rowOffsets[coordinates[0] + 1] - rowOffset;
+            const size_t columnOffset = columnOffsets[coordinates[1]];
+            const size_t columnCount = columnOffsets[coordinates[1] + 1] - columnOffset;
+
+            if (rank == 0) {
+                copyGlobalBlockToLocal(globalDistance, localDistance, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                       columnOffset, columnCount);
+            } else {
+                std::vector<unsigned int> packed(rowCount * columnCount);
+                copyGlobalBlockToLocal(globalDistance, packed, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                       columnOffset, columnCount);
+                sendUnsigned(packed.data(), packed.size(), rank, 0, grid);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        receiveUnsigned(localDistance.data(), localDistance.size(), 0, 0, grid);
+    }
+}
+
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance,
+                                                const size_t numNodes,

+                                                const std::vector<size_t>& columnOffsets,

+            receiveUnsigned(packed.data(), packed.size(), rank, 1, grid);
+            copyLocalBlockToGlobal(packed, globalDistance, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                   columnOffset, columnCount);
         }

+    return globalDistance;
+}

+void initializeLocalPathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t localSourceCount,
+                               const size_t localSourceOffset,

+        std::fill_n(path.data() + localSource * localDestinationCount, localDestinationCount,
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(localSourceOffset + localSource));

+std::vector<size_t> makeBlockOffsets(const size_t total, const int parts) {
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets(static_cast<size_t>(parts) + 1, 0);
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+
+    for (int part = 0; part < parts; ++part) {
+        offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part) + 1] =
+            offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part)] + base +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(part) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return offsets;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const std::vector<size_t>& offsets, const size_t index) {
+    return static_cast<int>(std::upper_bound(offsets.begin(), offsets.end(), index) -
+                            offsets.begin()) -
+           1;
+}

+void sendUnsigned(const unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int destination, const int tag,
+                  MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, destination, tag,
+                 communicator);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void receiveUnsigned(unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void copyGlobalBlockToLocal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& global,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& local, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes), columnCount,
+                    local.data() + localRow * columnCount);
+    }
+}
+

+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& global, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;

+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(local.data() + localRow * columnCount, columnCount,
+                    global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes));
+    }

+std::vector<size_t> makeBlockOffsets(const size_t total, const int parts) {
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets(static_cast<size_t>(parts) + 1, 0);
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+
+    for (int part = 0; part < parts; ++part) {
+        offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part) + 1] =
+            offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part)] + base +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(part) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return offsets;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const std::vector<size_t>& offsets, const size_t index) {
+    return static_cast<int>(std::upper_bound(offsets.begin(), offsets.end(), index) -
+                            offsets.begin()) -
+           1;
+}
+
+void sendUnsigned(const unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int destination, const int tag,
+                  MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, destination, tag,
+                 communicator);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void receiveUnsigned(unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void copyGlobalBlockToLocal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& global,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& local, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes), columnCount,
+                    local.data() + localRow * columnCount);
+    }
+}
+
+void copyLocalBlockToGlobal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& global, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(local.data() + localRow * columnCount, columnCount,
+                    global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes));
+    }
+}

+void distributeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance, const size_t numNodes,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& rowOffsets,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& columnOffsets, MPI_Comm grid,
+                              const int gridRank, const int gridSize) {

+        std::vector<unsigned int> globalDistance(numNodes * numNodes);
+        initializeDistanceMatrix(globalDistance, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);

+                copyGlobalBlockToLocal(globalDistance, packed, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                       columnOffset, columnCount);
+                sendUnsigned(packed.data(), packed.size(), rank, 0, grid);
+            }

+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance,

+void initializeLocalPathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t localSourceCount,

+    const size_t localColumnOffset = columnOffsets[coordinates[1]];
+    const size_t localColumnCount = columnOffsets[coordinates[1] + 1] - localColumnOffset;

+    std::vector<unsigned int> path(localRowCount * localColumnCount);
+    distributeDistanceMatrix(distance, numNodes, rowOffsets, columnOffsets, grid, gridRank,
+                             gridSize);
+    initializeLocalPathMatrix(path, localRowCount, localRowOffset, localColumnCount);

+    if (gridRank == 0) {
+        printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
+    }

+    MPI_Barrier(grid);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();

+                             localColumnOffset, localColumnCount, rowOffsets, columnOffsets,
+                             coordinates[0], coordinates[1], columnCommunicator, rowCommunicator);

+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsedSeconds, &elapsedSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, grid);
+
+    if (gridRank == 0) {
+        const long elapsedMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
+        printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", elapsedMilliseconds);
+        const double operations = static_cast<double>(numNodes) * numNodes * numNodes;
+        const double gops = elapsedSeconds > 0.0 ? operations / elapsedSeconds / 1e9 : 0.0;
+        printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gops);

+    if (printResults || validate) {
+        globalDistance = gatherDistanceMatrix(distance, numNodes, rowOffsets, columnOffsets, grid,
+                                              gridRank, gridSize);
+    }

+    if (gridRank == 0 && printResults) {
+        print_results_int(globalDistance, "DistanceMatrix");

+std::vector<size_t> makeBlockOffsets(const size_t total, const int parts) {
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets(static_cast<size_t>(parts) + 1, 0);
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+
+    for (int part = 0; part < parts; ++part) {
+        offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part) + 1] =
+            offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part)] + base +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(part) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return offsets;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const std::vector<size_t>& offsets, const size_t index) {
+    return static_cast<int>(std::upper_bound(offsets.begin(), offsets.end(), index) -
+                            offsets.begin()) -
+           1;
+}

+void sendUnsigned(const unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int destination, const int tag,
+                  MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, destination, tag,
+                 communicator);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void receiveUnsigned(unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void copyGlobalBlockToLocal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& global,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& local, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes), columnCount,
+                    local.data() + localRow * columnCount);
+    }
+}
+
+void copyLocalBlockToGlobal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& global, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(local.data() + localRow * columnCount, columnCount,
+                    global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes));
+    }

+std::vector<size_t> makeBlockOffsets(const size_t total, const int parts) {
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets(static_cast<size_t>(parts) + 1, 0);
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+
+    for (int part = 0; part < parts; ++part) {
+        offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part) + 1] =
+            offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part)] + base +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(part) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return offsets;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const std::vector<size_t>& offsets, const size_t index) {
+    return static_cast<int>(std::upper_bound(offsets.begin(), offsets.end(), index) -
+                            offsets.begin()) -
+           1;
+}

+void sendUnsigned(const unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int destination, const int tag,
+                  MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, destination, tag,
+                 communicator);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void receiveUnsigned(unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void copyGlobalBlockToLocal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& global,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& local, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes), columnCount,
+                    local.data() + localRow * columnCount);
+    }
+}
+
+void copyLocalBlockToGlobal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& global, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(local.data() + localRow * columnCount, columnCount,
+                    global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes));
+    }
+}

codex
Implemented MPI-only distributed Floyd–Warshall.

- 2-D process-grid decomposition with row/column pivot broadcasts for scalable distributed-memory execution: [floydwarshall.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/floydwarshall_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_mpi_r5/floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp:186)
- Preserved deterministic initialization, path updates, validation, and rank-0 result hashing/output.

+std::vector<size_t> makeBlockOffsets(const size_t total, const int parts) {
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets(static_cast<size_t>(parts) + 1, 0);
+    const size_t base = total / static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+    const size_t remainder = total % static_cast<size_t>(parts);
+
+    for (int part = 0; part < parts; ++part) {
+        offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part) + 1] =
+            offsets[static_cast<size_t>(part)] + base +
+            (static_cast<size_t>(part) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    return offsets;
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const std::vector<size_t>& offsets, const size_t index) {
+    return static_cast<int>(std::upper_bound(offsets.begin(), offsets.end(), index) -
+                            offsets.begin()) -
+           1;
+}
+
+void sendUnsigned(const unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int destination, const int tag,
+                  MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, destination, tag,
+                 communicator);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void receiveUnsigned(unsigned int* data, size_t count, const int source, const int tag,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxCount = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const size_t chunk = std::min(count - offset, maxCount);
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_UNSIGNED, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        offset += chunk;
+    }
+}
+
+void copyGlobalBlockToLocal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& global,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& local, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes), columnCount,
+                    local.data() + localRow * columnCount);
+    }
+}
+
+void copyLocalBlockToGlobal(const std::vector<unsigned int>& local,
+                            std::vector<unsigned int>& global, const size_t numNodes,
+                            const size_t rowOffset, const size_t rowCount,
+                            const size_t columnOffset, const size_t columnCount) {
+    if (rowCount == 0 || columnCount == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rowCount; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = rowOffset + localRow;
+        std::copy_n(local.data() + localRow * columnCount, columnCount,
+                    global.data() + idx2(columnOffset, globalRow, numNodes));
+    }
+}

+void distributeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& localDistance, const size_t numNodes,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& rowOffsets,
+                              const std::vector<size_t>& columnOffsets, MPI_Comm grid,
+                              const int gridRank, const int gridSize) {
+    if (gridRank == 0) {
+        std::vector<unsigned int> globalDistance(numNodes * numNodes);
+        initializeDistanceMatrix(globalDistance, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);

+                copyGlobalBlockToLocal(globalDistance, packed, numNodes, rowOffset, rowCount,
+                                       columnOffset, columnCount);
+                sendUnsigned(packed.data(), packed.size(), rank, 0, grid);
+            }
